{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"chews","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"chewing","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"chewed","tags":["past"]},{"form":"chewed","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"chewn","tags":["participle","past","rare"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Make sure to chew thoroughly, and don't talk with your mouth full!","type":"example"},{"text":"The steak was tough to chew as it had been cooked too long.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To crush with the teeth by repeated closing and opening of the jaws; done to food to soften it and break it down by the action of saliva before it is swallowed."]},{"examples":[{"text":"He keeps his feed in steel drums to prevent the mice from chewing holes in the feed-sacks.","type":"example"},{"text":"The harsh desert wind and sand had chewed the stump into ragged strips of wood.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To grind, tear, or otherwise degrade or demolish something with teeth or as with teeth."]},{"glosses":["The condition of something being torn or ground up mechanically."],"tags":["informal","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The professor stood at the blackboard, chalk in hand, and chewed the question the student had asked.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To think about something; to ponder; to chew over."],"tags":["informal"]},{"examples":[{"text":"To reduce cancer risk, it is best that you neither chew nor smoke.","type":"example"},{"text":"He'd been chewing for many years by the time he first noticed some white plaques on his oral mucosa.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To chew tobacco."]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/tʃuː/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/tʃʉw/","tags":["British","Southern","Standard"]},{"ipa":"/tʃɪʊ̯/","tags":["Canada","Wales"]},{"enpr":"cho͞o","tags":["Canada","US"]},{"ipa":"/t͡ʃ(j)u/","tags":["Canada","US"]}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"crush food with teeth prior to swallowing","word":"bite"},{"sense":"crush food with teeth prior to swallowing","word":"chavel"},{"sense":"crush food with teeth prior to swallowing","word":"chomp"},{"sense":"crush food with teeth prior to swallowing","word":"crunch"},{"sense":"crush food with teeth prior to swallowing","word":"masticate"},{"sense":"degrade or demolish as if with teeth","word":"grind"},{"sense":"degrade or demolish as if with teeth","word":"pulverize"},{"sense":"degrade or demolish as if with teeth","word":"rip"},{"sense":"degrade or demolish as if with teeth","word":"shred"},{"sense":"degrade or demolish as if with teeth","word":"tear"},{"sense":"think about","word":"contemplate"},{"sense":"think about","word":"ruminate"},{"sense":"think about","word":"mull"},{"sense":"think about","word":"muse"},{"sense":"think about","word":"ponder"}],"upstream_line":179917,"upstream_offset":858397607,"upstream_record_sha256":"7e81a0c4112777959b51dd97129bd3d91be89a97d7a9e6c52a9aac457f7f3b13","word":"chew"}

{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"dares","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"dare","tags":["modal","present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"dast","tags":["obsolete","present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"daring","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"dared","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"dared","tags":["past"]},{"form":"durst","tags":["obsolete","participle","past"]},{"form":"durst","tags":["obsolete","past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"I wouldn't dare (to) argue with my boss.","type":"example"},{"text":"No one dares (to) criticize his decisions.","type":"example"},{"text":"They daredn't tell me the truth, even though I dared (to) acknowledge my mistake to them.","type":"example"},{"text":"I daren't tell him I've had an affair with another man.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To have enough courage (to do something)."],"synonyms":[{"word":"venture"}],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I dare you to kiss that girl.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To defy or challenge (someone to do something)."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Will you dare death to reach your goal?","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To have enough courage to meet or do something, go somewhere, etc.; to face up to."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To terrify; to daunt."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To drive larks to the ground by scaring them (for instance, with mirrors or hawks) so they can be caught in nets."],"tags":["archaic","transitive"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/dɛə/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/dɛ(ə)ɹ/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":13291,"upstream_offset":85682901,"upstream_record_sha256":"e81c517701d5cb3e59fa004734be76bf29e58df24b3047346e6ed7aabe60cde1","word":"dare"}
